            let el = document.createElement("link");
            el.setAttribute("rel", isPrefetch ? "prefetch" : "preload");
            el.setAttribute("as", "script");
            el.setAttribute("href", src);
            el.setAttribute("fetchPriority", fetchPriority);

          }
          window.fetchCSS = {
            load: function(href) {
                const link = document.createElement("link");
                link.rel = "stylesheet";
                link.href = href;
                document.head.appendChild(link);
            },
            preload: function(href) {
                const link = document.createElement("link");
                link.rel = "preload";
                link.as = "style";
                link.href = href;
                document.head.appendChild(link);
                return link;
            },
            prefetch: function(href) {
                const link = document.createElement("link");
                link.rel = "prefetch";
                link.href = href;
                document.head.appendChild(link);
            },
            loadNonBlocking: function(href) {
                const preloadLink = this.preload(href);
                preloadLink.onload = () => this.load(href);
            }

            const abExpsVariants =  [];
            const abExpMriSync = groupedConfigs.abExpMriSync || [];
            function assignToBucket (weights, cookiePrefix) {
              const expt = cookiePrefix || "V"
              const random = Math.random();
              for(let i = 0 ; i < weights.length ; i++){
                if(random >= weights[i] && random <= weights[i+1]  ){
                  return expt+i;
                }   
              }
            }
        
            function computeWeights (originalTraffic, bucketTraffic ) {
            
              /*
                originalTraffic : Holding traffic for which cookie will not be set
                bucketTraffic: Array of split % within experiment traffic
                eg: [0.33, 0.33, 0.34]  =>  This means the experiment traffic will be equally divided into 3 parts
                Sum of all elements in bucketTraffic + originalTraffic should be equal to 1 (100% users set)
              */
              let abWeights = [originalTraffic];
              let minRange = originalTraffic;
              for( let i = 0; i< bucketTraffic.length; i++) {
                const maxRange = minRange + bucketTraffic[i];
                abWeights.push(maxRange);
                minRange = maxRange;
              }
              return abWeights;
            }
        
        
            const addToExpsList = (cookieName, val) => {
              if(!abExpsList.includes(cookieName) && abExpMriSync.includes(cookieName)){
                abExpsList.push(cookieName)
                abExpsVariants.push(cookieName+':' + val)
              }
            }
            if( groupedConfigs.multipleAbData &&  groupedConfigs.multipleAbData.length){
              for(let i = 0; i <  groupedConfigs.multipleAbData.length; i++ ){
                const abDataItem =  groupedConfigs.multipleAbData[i];
                let abWeights = computeWeights(abDataItem.originalTraffic, abDataItem.bucketTraffic);
                const pageName = 'home';
                let cookieValue = getCookie(abDataItem.cookieName)
                if(abDataItem.page === pageName &&  !cookieValue){
        
                  var abBucket = assignToBucket(abWeights, abDataItem.cookiePrefix)
                  abBucket && setCookie(abDataItem.cookieName, abBucket, (abDataItem.expiry || 14))
                  abBucket && addToExpsList(abDataItem.cookieName, abBucket )
                  try {
                    if (groupedConfigs.enableUIFacelift &&  abDataItem.cookieName === 'uiFaceliftAB' && abBucket === "V2" &&  (pageName === "search" || pageName === "payment")){
                      fetchCSS.loadNonBlocking('/public/stylesheet/interFont.a395dc25.css?root=rbus')

              console.log("moE sdk initialized")
              const me_uidhash = getCookie("me_uidhash") || null;
              const isMoeUserIdAdded = getCookie('isMoeUserIdAdded');
              const isMoeEnabled = getCookie("isMoeEnabled");
              if(me_uidhash && !isMoeUserIdAdded && !isMoeEnabled) {
                Moengage.add_unique_user_id(me_uidhash);
                setCookie('isMoeUserIdAdded',true);
              }
              setCookie("isMoeEnabled",true);
            }
            //clear initMoe listener
            if(shouldClearListener){
              window.ontouchstart = null;
            }
          }
          const enableMoeTimeout = groupedConfigs &&  groupedConfigs.enableMoeTimeout;
          const timeoutVal = groupedConfigs && groupedConfigs.moeTimeout;
            if(enableMoeTimeout) {
              setTimeout(() => {
                moeTrigger();
              }, (timeoutVal || 3000));
            } else {
              moeTrigger();

<p>Booking a bus ticket online on the redBus app or website is very simple. You can download the redBus app or visit redbus.in and enter your source, destination &amp; travel date to check the top-rated bus services available. You can then compare bus prices, user ratings &amp; amenities, select your preferred seat, boarding &amp; dropping points and pay using multiple payment options like UPI, debit or credit card, net banking and more. With redBus, get assured safe &amp; secure payment methods and guaranteed travel with the best seat and bus operator of your choice. Once the bus booking payment is confirmed, all you have to do is pack your bags and get ready to travel with the m-ticket, which you can show to the bus operator on your mobile before boarding the bus. With redDeals, you can also get exclusive &amp; additional discounts on your online bus ticket booking. You will get 24/7 customer support on call, chat &amp; help to resolve all your queries in English &amp; local languages.</p>
<p>redBus offers Primo bus services, specially curated by redBus, which have highly rated buses and best-in-class service. With Primo by redBus, you can be assured of safe, comfortable, and on-time bus service at no additional cost. With multiple boarding and dropping points available across all major cities in India, you can select at your convenience and enjoy a smooth travel experience.</p>
<p>redBus operates in six countries, including India, Malaysia, Singapore, Indonesia, Peru, and Colombia. Through its website and app, it has sold over 220 million bus tickets worldwide. With over 36 million satisfied customers, redBus is committed to providing its users with a world-class online bus booking experience.</p>
<p>redBus offers bus tickets from some of the top private bus operators, such as Orange Travels, VRL Travels, SRS Travels, Chartered Bus, and Praveen Travels, and state government bus operators, such as APSRTC, TSRTC, GSRTC, Kerala RTC, TNSTC, RSRTC, UPSRTC, and more. Family and friends may also check the bus position to schedule pick-ups and ensure safety.</p></span></div></div>  <div class="D119_heading accordionItem faq-hide  " style="padding: 1em 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #ccc; font-size: 1em; color: #3e3e52;" data-autoid="" faqid="8" itemscope="" itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question"><div itemprop="name" style="color: #3e3e52; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0 0 0.5em; font-size: 1em; font-weight: normal;"><span class="seo-srp-ques-part" style="font-weight: bold;"> Do I need to create an account on the redBus site to book my bus ticket?  </span><span faqspanid="8" class="icon icon-faq_plus faqIcon" style="display: flex; align-items: center; color: #000;"></span></div><div class="accordionItemContent seo-faq-answer seo-srp-faq-answer" itemscope="" itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer"><span class="D119_ans" itemprop="text" style="color: var(--clr-gray-4);"> <p>No, you don’t have to create an account on redBus to book your bus ticket. But it is advisable to do so to accelerate the process next time you want to book a bus ticket. Also, redBus has many discounts and offers on bus bookings that you can easily access if you have an account with us.</p></span></div></div>  <div class="D119_heading accordionItem faq-hide  " style="padding: 1em 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #ccc; font-size: 1em; color: #3e3e52;" data-autoid="" faqid="9" itemscope="" itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question"><div itemprop="name" style="color: #3e3e52; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0 0 0.5em; font-size: 1em; font-weight: normal;"><span class="seo-srp-ques-part" style="font-weight: bold;"> Why book bus tickets online on redBus? </span><span faqspanid="9" class="icon icon-faq_plus faqIcon" style="display: flex; align-items: center; color: #000;"></span></div><div class="accordionItemContent seo-faq-answer seo-srp-faq-answer" itemscope="" itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer"><span class="D119_ans" itemprop="text" style="color: var(--clr-gray-4);"> <ul><li>Booking bus tickets online on redBus is increasingly becoming the preferred choice for travellers due to its many advantages over traditional methods. With redBus. Here are a few reasons to book bus tickets online on redBus:&nbsp;</li><li>&nbsp;Customers can book their bus tickets effortlessly from the comfort of their homes, avoiding the inconvenience of standing in long lines at bus stations or travel agencies.&nbsp;</li><li>Online bus booking offers the luxury of comparing different bus schedules and operators. It also presents discounts and exclusive deals, resulting in significant savings.</li><li>Payment security is another notable feature of online booking, which ensures that your financial information is well-protected against fraud.</li><li>Additionally, customers can pick their seats, providing a customized travel experience.&nbsp;</li><li>Online bus booking platforms provide real-time updates about bus timetable changes, including delays or cancellations, enabling better planning.</li><li>The convenience doesn`t stop here; travellers can even compare onboard amenities like charging points or snacks, further enhancing the travel experience.</li></ul></span></div></div>  <div class="D119_heading accordionItem faq-hide  " style="padding: 1em 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #ccc; font-size: 1em; color: #3e3e52;" data-autoid="" faqid="10" itemscope="" itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question"><div itemprop="name" style="color: #3e3e52; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0 0 0.5em; font-size: 1em; font-weight: normal;"><span class="seo-srp-ques-part" style="font-weight: bold;"> What`s New in Bus Booking on redBus? </span><span faqspanid="10" class="icon icon-faq_plus faqIcon" style="display: flex; align-items: center; color: #000;"></span></div><div class="accordionItemContent seo-faq-answer seo-srp-faq-answer" itemscope="" itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer"><span class="D119_ans" itemprop="text" style="color: var(--clr-gray-4);"> <p><a href="https://www.redbus.in/primo/primo-service" target="_self">Primo Bus </a>Ticket: redBus has launched Primo bus services, where passengers can enjoy travelling in high-rated buses with best-in-class bus services. Primo Buses` online booking experience can benefit passengers from hygiene standards to on-time service and comfort.</p></span></div></div>  <div class="D119_heading accordionItem faq-hide  " style="padding: 1em 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #ccc; font-size: 1em; color: #3e3e52;" data-autoid="" faqid="11" itemscope="" itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question"><div itemprop="name" style="color: #3e3e52; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0 0 0.5em; font-size: 1em; font-weight: normal;"><span class="seo-srp-ques-part" style="font-weight: bold;"> I`ve lost my ticket. What should I do now? </span><span faqspanid="11" class="icon icon-faq_plus faqIcon" style="display: flex; align-items: center; color: #000;"></span></div><div class="accordionItemContent seo-faq-answer seo-srp-faq-answer" itemscope="" itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer"><span class="D119_ans" itemprop="text" style="color: var(--clr-gray-4);"> <p>A copy of the bus ticket would have been sent to your registered email when you booked the bus ticket online. Please take a printout of that mail and produce it when boarding. If you have not received the ticket e-mail, please call any of our call centres, and our executive will resend you a copy by mail.</p></span></div></div>  <div class="D119_heading accordionItem faq-hide  " style="padding: 1em 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #ccc; font-size: 1em; color: #3e3e52;" data-autoid="" faqid="12" itemscope="" itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question"><div itemprop="name" style="color: #3e3e52; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0 0 0.5em; font-size: 1em; font-weight: normal;"><span class="seo-srp-ques-part" style="font-weight: bold;"> What is an mTicket? </span><span faqspanid="12" class="icon icon-faq_plus faqIcon" style="display: flex; align-items: center; color: #000;"></span></div><div class="accordionItemContent seo-faq-answer seo-srp-faq-answer" itemscope="" itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer"><span class="D119_ans" itemprop="text" style="color: var(--clr-gray-4);"> <p>A mTicket is an SMS sent to your mobile to book bus tickets with select private bus operators. This SMS has the TIN, PNR number, and other ticket-related information. It can be used to board the bus. Please note that not all operators accept mTickets. To know which operators are mTicket enabled, look for the mTicket icon under the mTicket column while searching for buses.</p></span></div></div>  <div class="D119_heading accordionItem faq-hide  " style="padding: 1em 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #ccc; font-size: 1em; color: #3e3e52;" data-autoid="" faqid="13" itemscope="" itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question"><div itemprop="name" style="color: #3e3e52; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0 0 0.5em; font-size: 1em; font-weight: normal;"><span class="seo-srp-ques-part" style="font-weight: bold;"> How to cancel bus ticket online on redBus? </span><span faqspanid="13" class="icon icon-faq_plus faqIcon" style="display: flex; align-items: center; color: #000;"></span></div><div class="accordionItemContent seo-faq-answer seo-srp-faq-answer" itemscope="" itemprop="acceptedAnswer" itemtype="https://schema.org/Answer"><span class="D119_ans" itemprop="text" style="color: var(--clr-gray-4);"> <p>To cancel the bus ticket online on redBus, click the cancellation link provided on our home page. Enter your bus ticket number and email ID provided at the time of bus booking and click cancel the ticket.</p></span></div></div>  <div class="D119_heading accordionItem faq-hide  " style="padding: 1em 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #ccc; font-size: 1em; color: #3e3e52;" data-autoid="" faqid="14" itemscope="" itemprop="mainEntity" itemtype="https://schema.org/Question"><div itemprop="name" style="color: #3e3e52; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0 0 0.5em; font-size: 1em; font-weight: normal;"><span class="seo-srp-ques-part" style="font-weight: bold;"> I missed the bus.   window.scroll = resetCookie;
  window.ontouchstart = resetCookie;
  window.onclick = resetCookie;
  window.onkeypress = resetCookie;
  
  function resetCookie() {
    const cookies = document.cookie.split(';').reduce((prev, current) => {
      const [name, value] = current.split('=').map(part => unescape(part.trim()));
      if (name && value) {
        prev[name] = value;
      }
      return prev;
    }, {});

    // const writeCookie = (key, value, expMins=null) => {
    //   let ex = new Date();
    //   expMins = expMins || (365 * 24 * 60);
    //   ex = new Date(ex.getTime() + expMins * 60000);
    //   value = escape(value) + ((ex === null) || expMins === -1 ? '' : '; expires=' + new Date(ex).toUTCString()) + '; path=/';
    //   if (location.protocol !== 'https:') {
    //       document.cookie = key + '=' + value;
    //   } else {
    //       document.cookie = key + '=' + value + ';secure';
    //   }	
    // }
    let transaction;
    let mriSessionId = cookies["mriSessionId"] || '';
    let mriClientIdSetDateString = cookies["mriClientIdSetDate"] || '';
    if(window.elasticApm && groupedConfigs && groupedConfigs.enableMRIValidityTracking) {
      transaction = window.elasticApm.startTransaction('MRI ID Validity', 'custom');
      transaction.addLabels({'sessionIdBefore': mriSessionId, 'allCookies': document.cookie});
    }

    if(mriClientIdSetDateString) {
      const curDate = new Date();
      const setDate = new Date(mriClientIdSetDateString);
      if(!((setDate.getDate() === curDate.getDate()) && (setDate.getMonth() === curDate.getMonth()) && (setDate.getFullYear() === curDate.getFullYear()))) {
        const mriClientId = cookies["mriClientId"] || '';
        setCookie("mriClientId", mriClientId);
        setCookie("mriClientIdSetDate", curDate);
      }
    }
    mriSessionId && setCookie("mriSessionId", mriSessionId, 30);
    if(transaction) {
      transaction.addLabels({'sessionIdAfter': cookies["mriSessionId"] || ''});
      setTimeout(() => {
        transaction.end();
      }, 500);
    }
  }</script><script>setCookie('commitHash', document.getElementById('commitHash').innerHTML)</script>  <script async="" src="https://cdn.debugbear.com/p6uGb7ucyd07.js"></script>   <link href="/manifest.json" fetchpriority="low" as="fetch"><iframe allow="join-ad-interest-group" data-tagging-id="G-W2P7QGN8S2" data-load-time="1735835819404" height="0" width="0" src="https://td.doubleclick.net/td/ga/rul?tid=G-W2P7QGN8S2&amp;gacid=1982426647.1735835819&amp;gtm=45je4cc1v9105410534z86912255za200zb6912255&amp;dma=0&amp;gcd=13l3l3l3l1l1&amp;npa=0&amp;pscdl=noapi&amp;aip=1&amp;fledge=1&amp;frm=0&amp;tag_exp=101925629~102067555~102067808~102081485~102198178&amp;z=235796725" style="display: none; visibility: hidden;"></iframe><noscript><img src="https://www.redbus.in/akam/13/pixel_725183c7?a=dD00ZjY5YTgzYzk2MTFhNGQxN2Q0MzA2N2UwYTA0MmExOGZkZjQyZWZiJmpzPW9mZg==" style="visibility: hidden; position: absolute; left: -999px; top: -999px;" /></noscript></body></html>
